## Qualifications

- Selected GDM decoded literals exactly reproduce the September 28/September 24 pinned asset, with unversioned App data. The complete Order 94 40k.app Heroic Intervention observation replaces the truncated GDM row; original blocks remain retained.
- Source transcription equivalence and semantic execution are separate. Runtime source receipts retain independent load and execution statuses. Narrow excerpts do not certify omitted clauses.
- Order 87 mixed random Movement grouping and Order 92 random melee weapon commitment remain explicitly provisional owner conventions; neither is promoted to source-certified semantics.
- C12-04 uses owner-confirmed official App v946 Engaging-only forced fights. C18-07 uses passenger post-placement engagement, never inherited Transport enemy engagements.
- The historical Order 84 and Order 95 negative audits, all 20 September 10 dispositions and prior findings remain immutable. PFINAL must independently re-audit all clauses after prerequisites merge.
- Geometric/domain assertions, facade submissions, invalid inputs, replay and viewer evidence are labeled by their actual scope. An assertion link is not by itself a universal consumer-path certificate.
- Complete-game performance remains deferred under Order 32 and is not certified by this evidence-only change.

## Required follow-ups before PFINAL

- **C03-06 / P03F:** An impossible complete setup leaves the unit selected and trapped in placement retries. Restore selected-to-move eligibility as required, preserve its prior location, and expose legal reselection/Remain Stationary/retry through shared facade authority, historical restore and replay.
- **C04-07 / P04F:** A weaponless Fight activation emits unit_has_fought despite resolving no melee attacks. Separate selection completion from actual fought status across ordinary and retained hosts without allowing an extra activation; authenticate the distinction in effects, restore, viewers and replay.
- **C04-08 / P04G:** PRECISION unnecessarily separates otherwise identical attacks against a rules unit with no CHARACTER. Make shared grouping depend on applicable effects against the selected target, preserving deterministic group identity, physical weapons and target replacement.
- **C04-09 / P04H:** The same enemy model is incorrectly required to satisfy range and visibility. Permit a visible model and a different in-range model in the same target rules unit, retaining complete visibility witnesses, target validation and shared ordinary/reaction attack authority.
- **C06-04 / P06D:** A mixed MOUNTED/VEHICLE rules unit takes three hazard mortal wounds although not every model is MONSTER/VEHICLE. Apply the all-model requirement through shared unit Hazard/Hazardous allocation, including mixed and attached groups; retain the distinct correct INFANTRY/VEHICLE FAQ case.
- **C12-05 / P12C:** Pile In compares minimum target distance rather than preserving the initially closest selected target, and can leave a model unengaged despite an attainable legal engaged endpoint. Share authenticated per-model reachability and closest-target constraints with existing movement owners; preserve coherency, full PathWitnesses, unresolved-proof failure and replay.
- **C12-06 / P12D:** An unqualified extra Pile In distance currently applies to Overrun during the Fight step. Scope it to the Pile In step unless the source explicitly extends it, and verify other extra-movement families and historical phase/step context.
- **C13-02 / P13B:** Complete selected terrain semantics: retain contact during climbing, block forbidden Dense-floor traversal, treat enclosed Solid windows as volume for visibility and endpoints, and represent source Exposed traversal explicitly. Repair shared physical predicates and typed classification with witnessed paths and continuous-volume cases.
- **C14-04 / P14B:** The objective-control consumer treats an OC0 unit as controlling because a different same-player unit supplies positive control. Require the selected controlling unit to contribute OC while preserving the controller-first result and shared Stratagem/mission consumers.
- **C16-04 / P16C:** Becoming Battle-shocked leaves an active Action STARTED instead of interrupting it. Apply the shared interruption authority and support an explicit source permission to perform the Action while shocked; preserve original restrictions, movement interruption, historical state and replay.
- **C09-04 / P09D:** The unqualified friendly-VEHICLE transit permission is restricted to Normal/Advance; the retained Fall Back witness is rejected. Apply the source scope across eligible move families through shared collision legality without granting enemy or forbidden endpoint transit.
- **C24-12 / P24L:** A melee declaration can omit an eligible equipped Extra Attacks weapon. Require all such weapons alongside the permitted primary choice, preserving physical weapon ownership, random attack commitments and typed retry/replay.
- **C24-13 / P24M:** Blast is fixed to one extra attack per five target models and cannot represent Blast X. Add the source-backed parameter and generic attack-count consumer, preserving selected-target timing, attached counts and shared Shooting/reaction paths.
- **C18-11 / P18J:** The Core embark contract requires invented Normal/Advance/Fall Back movement for an explicit no-movement embark permission. Represent the genuine source context, preserve post-disembark permission and cargo authority, and expose it through the same finite/proposal contract. Loading a named faction ability remains outside this Core audit.
- **C02-11 / P02K:** A source-dash Movement model can Advance because the bonus is added to its numeric zero budget. Preserve absent-M immobility distinctly from numeric zero across ordinary and reactive movement families while retaining explicit setup permissions, full paths, restore and replay.
- **C06-05 / P06E:** Additional mortal wounds have no source-backed attack producer: the sole deferred-mortal constructor replaces damage for Devastating Wounds. Add a real generic additional-mortal consumer preserving the originating attack identity, ordinary damage ordering, allocation and replay; do not infer this clause from damage replacement.
- **C06-06 / P06F:** Mortal-wound recipient selection lacks a generic source-backed allocation-trigger execution path. Its existing audit occurrence is emitted only when optional/multiple Feel No Pain pauses. Expose the actual Select Model boundary consistently to real source-linked consumers while preserving finite recipient choice, allocation priority and replay.
- **C19-02 / P19B:** An ordinary destroyed conferring model loses its attached-unit ability during the unfinished attacking-unit sequence. Preserve attack-scoped source presence until that sequence completes, then expire it through the shared ability/catalog consumer; distinguish existing special Fight/Shoot-on-death retention and verify effect lifetimes through restore and replay.
- **C20-03 / P20C:** Authenticate a real Movement-phase reposition source and its shared provider, then prove accepted prior movement followed by same-phase reposition, restore and replay. Existing providers and retained-history tests cover later Turn End; they do not establish this selected permission, and the shared state owner has not been shown to reject it.
- **C22-03 / P22C:** Authenticate a real Psychic ability damage source and propagate its structured attack classification into the shared mortal-wound and Feel No Pain consumers. The retained Cabal probe loses Psychic-only protection, but its named ability Psychic title is not transcribed in the pinned official update; resolve that source identity before certifying the named failure.
- **C08-04 / P08C:** A supported unqualified Command-phase clause has no registered consumer; the facade advances to Movement without its effect while the explicit start-of-Command control executes. Add source-backed generic Command-abilities step discovery and resolution after Core CP/Battle-shock and before end-phase effects, preserving decisions, replay and distinct timing boundaries.
- **C01-11 / P01K:** Effects with no explicit duration are discarded by the generic persisting-effect owner. Derive the selected default expiry from the triggering period, or the end of the phase in which it was given when no trigger period is specified, and carry the effect into actual modifier consumers, preserving turn ownership, boundary expiry, source identity and replay.
- **C02-12 / P02L:** The canonical factory fixture omits the canonicalized datasheet-name keyword. Trace official provider normalization and establish the selected name-as-keyword invariant through shared catalog identity and unit/model queries; the diagnostic establishes only the fixture/factory boundary and does not claim an official-provider defect.
- **C02-13 / P02M:** The selected Core nested-condition exemplar is typed unsupported at source compilation. Authenticate and execute a real source-backed nested branch through generic RuleIR, preserving the outer eligibility condition when the stronger inner effect applies; a parser rejection does not establish incorrectly applied rerolls.
- **C02-14 / P02N:** An implicit friendly-keyword target without an explicit model/unit noun remains unsupported residual language. Normalize the selected default unit subject at the data boundary and verify real source-backed model/unit keyword consumers without runtime text parsing.
- **C03-07 / P03G:** A cyclic exchange of two model positions is accepted although neither model can move first without crossing the other initial base. Enforce the selected sequential model-movement invariant with complete witnesses and authenticated occupancy order; preserve group coherency, legal friendly transit and deterministic replay.
- **C03-08 / P03H:** An all-model Infiltrators redeployment is rejected outside its deployment zone through the actual facade placement request. Apply source-backed Infiltrators permissions at redeployment as at initial setup, retaining enemy distances, full model geometry, invalid atomicity, restore and replay.
- **C03-09 / P03I:** Setup accepts avoidable body overhang because it tests support bases against the deployment region. Apply the selected whole-model fit and only its genuine impossible-fit exception across deployment and reserve setup; the retained probe demonstrates deployment and explicitly leaves the shared reserve counterpart for the same geometry-owner audit.
- **C03-10 / P03J:** A legal witnessed Fly Advance crosses enemy Engagement Range and ends unengaged, but its exact facade checkpoint fails Advance-history restoration. Authenticate Fly movement modes in shared boundary history without weakening movement evidence, and cover restored projections and exact replay.
- **C03-11 / P03K:** A coherency casualty counts as destroyed but is absent from per-model secondary scoring while its unit survives. Record every eligible destroyed model in shared mission destruction history, preserving objective-control-first ordering, physical ownership and no duplicate unit completion; cover actual score ledgers, restore and replay across destruction causes.
- **C01-12 / P01L:** Establish a real source-backed as-close-as-possible objective movement consumer and its typed closest-legal endpoint constraint. The selected Core definition has no authenticated active consumer in this inventory; Consolidation explicitly requires only closer when unreachable, and load-only faction placeholders cannot certify this stronger rule. Preserve that distinction and prove reachable/unreachable geometry, full witnesses and replay.
- **C18-12 / P18K:** Rapid and Tactical disembark reject an analytically wholly-within exact three-inch circular-base boundary because the shared containment owner buffers polygon approximations. Use the authoritative model geometry for inclusive containment and test exact, below and above boundaries across disembark modes, with facade invalid atomicity, restore and replay.
- **C02-15 / P02O:** Singular and plural keyword spellings normalize to different identities and the real typed unit gate rejects the plural of a present singular keyword. Normalize source-backed number aliases at the catalog/RuleIR boundary, preserve canonical runtime tokens and test both spellings through actual model/unit rule consumers; do not confuse model-owner label matching with keyword identity.
- **C03-12 / P03L:** Reconcile conditional during-turn oversized deployment restrictions with the setup-only deployment owner. Preserve the Order54 convention that pregame deployment creates no first-turn lock, and do not borrow the Strategic Reserves AIRCRAFT exception. Establish an applicable source-linked in-turn consumer before certifying the conditional activity restrictions; the retained boundary test does not assert a current gameplay violation.

Cogitated Need and Synaptic Goading contain objective as-close wording in load-only faction activation data; those placeholders do not establish Core execution. A real applicable source-linked consumer and witnessed closest-endpoint proof are required before certification.

The pinned gap_observations_timing.json and tests/order97_gap_probes_timing.py isolate the missing Command-body registration without claiming named faction support. Add the shared body-step consumer and verify its position after CP/Battle-shock and before end-phase effects.

Qualification: Evidence gap: The shared reposition owner preserves prior movement, and the existing authenticated tests prove that at a later Turn End. Current catalog ability removal routes are prebattle or turn-end providers; no source-backed Movement-phase reposition provider has been linked to an accepted post-move choice in this audit. This is an authenticated source/consumer coverage prerequisite, not a demonstrated rejection by the shared state owner. Establish a real source-backed Movement-phase consumer, accepted prior movement, same-phase reposition and replay before closure.

Qualification: Evidence gap: The actual Cabal Doombolt damage consumer retains a successful Psychic test in source context, but the shared mortal-wound owner has no Psychic classification input and excludes a Psychic-only Feel No Pain source. The retained two-branch probe contrasts an unconditional source with the otherwise identical Psychic-only source. The selected Core clause requires authenticated Psychic ability wounds to count as Psychic attacks; the pinned official Thousand Sons update refers to Doombolt but does not transcribe its own Psychic title, so that named source identity remains unverified. Establish that source evidence and propagate structured damage classification through the shared consumer before certification; the existing attack proof only covers an already-tagged weapon attack.
